# Actors Resource Assurance — Research Conclusions

## Ownership and Evidence Status

This is the single compiled owner of exploratory Actors resource-assurance conclusions. It is **not an Experiment Record**, a production price or a second backlog. [EXP-001](EXP-001.md) owns the physical baseline; [BACKLOG](../../../../../BACKLOG.md) owns open work. Specifications, code and tests retain project-owned truth. No build, test, CI or production acceptance operation depends on this document.

Raw receipt pages, their historical snapshot and the companion manifest are intentionally not retained. The observations below are reviewed, source-scoped conclusions, not a self-contained audit/replay dataset. Artifact identities distinguish the original coordinates; they do not make old measurements current. New production qualification must use applicable code and adequate current evidence, not this summary as a substitute for raw data.

## Current Decision Summary

| Question | Conclusion | Limit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Fresh-Cycle authority | Main-source classification and actual workers now authenticate before new execution and after Opening/late callbacks; permanent native regressions pass. | Source correction retained, not production-qualified; paid Run/prefix remains intact and affected physical bindings are open. |
| Complete I/refusal composition | Component-plus-AbortCycle-tail misses reached RetryLater preflight. Actual whole callers cover that branch. | No numerical I>K or architecture-impossibility result. |
| Corrected I/K observation | A source-matched corrected pair passes native SDK markers and diagnostic Wasm. | Observability/applicability, not conservative prices or same-domain K containment. |
| System quarter | Configured fixed declarations exceed the normative quarter on both axes. | Declaration nonconformance, not an observed physical block overrun. |
| Prefix credits | Governance/MQ register once; the economic freeze retains maxima. The joined candidate lacks complete Authorship ownership. | No arbitrary refund, phase subtraction, duplicate allowance or production credit. |
| Authorship domain | New header input, prior parent representation, root witness and block-data/PoV have distinct owners. | Finite classes and equal scalars do not certify a complete physical maximum. |

## Corrected I/K Research

I denotes the complete selected inspection/refusal encounter, including performed quote, RetryLater preflight, plan and reconciliation work. The independently observed K is a complete canonical zero-Step control caller. Comparing qualified I/K declarations requires the same supported domain and **both** RefTime and ProofSize; raw marker-time ordering is not that comparison.

| Preservation | Ordinary native/benchmark artifacts restored; production code, weights and Wasm unchanged. | An unsuccessful cache-restoration probe was rejected; no body tests ran for restoration. |

The economic-only Pipeline provider is a candidate port, not the live compiler; Creation-backed destruction remains separate. Raw extras stay generation-prohibited pending complete domains and conservative both-axis bounds.

Coordinator source checking rejects the over-broad reviewer wording that all policy/wait capability is unavailable: policy maximum/actual methods are present and ordinary policy Close consumes compiled plain/policy maxima, while financial waiting independently needs unavailable production supplements. The misleading benchmark-authority doc comment was corrected without changing executable bodies. This representation-only clarification neither rebinds the canary's source epoch nor requires another benchmark build; physical policy qualification remains open.

## Review Conclusions and Cache Safety

Two independent post-implementation reviews checked the projection, 264 source identities, thirteen then-retained archive texts, raw points and protected artifacts. They approved the finite diagnostic result, not prices. Their findings are retained here rather than as separate review transcripts.

| Finding | Resolution / interpretation |
| --- | --- |
| Medium: retained corrected workspace still aliases main Cargo target | Removed the owned `source/template/target` symlink after frozen review, without build/replay. The source remains unaccepted diagnostics, not a production build workspace. |
| Low: budget wording exceeds independently auditable enforcement | The recorded run set and executor receipts support cohort completion; they do not independently audit every outer timeout or exclude every possible unrecorded attempt. No violation was demonstrated. |

Same-layout source copies can alias shared Cargo fingerprints. Original-root Cargo initially returned the corrected binary with 657 filtered tests despite unchanged main source; exit zero did not establish restoration. Quarantining package fingerprints and rebuilding ordinary packages returned the exact original binary with 656 filtered tests and zero executed body tests. Future diagnostics need distinct output namespaces or explicit preserved/restored artifacts, not source-path assumptions.

No new physical samples, artifact issuance, price, quarter credit or release acceptance is selected.

## Authorship No-Author Entry-Domain Reduction

**Question:** May complete Authorship qualification drop discovery returning None because Aura always supplies an author? Only under a named entry/profile and explicit coherence premises. The registered Cumulus validation entry and direct Executive/runtime APIs are not the same boundary. This is source reduction, not a native accepted-block witness or a network-admission defect.

| Source branch | Authorship consequence | Exclusion boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Cached Author exists | Return cached account, then invoke callback | Canonical cold-cache lineage is separate from this generic supported path |
| No Aura pre-runtime item, first matching item fails Slot decoding, or Aura authority count decodes as zero | Aura FindAuthor returns None | AuraExt seal verification requires Some index before executing the block |
| Aura index exists but Session.Validators lacks that index | Session adapter returns None | AuraExt verifies an Aura key, not this separate account mapping; require roster coherence |
| Cold discovery returns None | No cache creation or callback; on_initialize still returns zero and on_finalize still kills cache | Zero return or absent callback declaration does not make discovery/wrapper/deletion free |

**Actual entry chain:** `runtime/src/lib.rs:276` registers AuraExt BlockExecutor for validate_block. ParachainSystem `validate_block/implementation.rs:251–271` invokes `E::verify_and_remove_seal` before `E::execute_verified_block`, in separate externalities/recorder scopes. AuraExt `lib.rs:132–174` requires one decodable Aura seal, a Some Aura author index, an indexed cached authority and successful signature verification. No index exits by panic before the Authorship hook. This guard has its own incurred-work/host owner; source placement supplies no CPU, witness discount or proof-floor bound.

**Narrow conditional exclusion:** The verified header's first Aura input must survive to Authorship, its Aura authority count must remain the same, and the selected index must exist in Session.Validators. Under these premises the configured Session adapter returns Some, so cold no-author is excluded. Current source supports the coherence argument: named genesis uses the same invulnerable sequence for Session keys; ordinary TrustedSet management returns Some roster; rotation installs validators from session_keys and Aura receives those keys. Truncation and the empty-update no-op have different consequences; the [empty-queue countercase](#authorship-empty-queue-coherence-countercase) defeats a general roster-coherence inference. Authorship precedes ordinary rotation. First rotation/queued-change, earlier cache/digest/roster mutation, upgrade/LP histories and actual executing-entry binding must stay explicit; this record does not certify their complete reachable-state induction or a generic-host invariant.

**Broader entry limit:** `apis.rs:230–235` delegates Core execute/initialize directly to Executive. Executive's ExecuteBlock seal method is empty; its initialization extracts pre-runtime digests and runs hooks without the AuraExt wrapper. Aura on_initialize tolerates missing/undecodable slot input by its no-update branch. Timestamp checks stored-slot consistency later, and the consensus hook checks relay-slot alignment later; neither is an entry-local proof that Authorship already returned Some. Thus no-author cannot be excluded from all initialization/API/helper attempts using the PVF guard. No successful whole block, admitted parent lineage or bypass of full validation is established here.

**Method decision:** Keep author-discovery None distinct from an absent recipient account: c=0/1/2 all prepare successful discovery. A future complete quote must either bind the narrower verified/coherent profile with its premises, or supply supported coverage/domination for retained None branches on both axes. Do not automatically add a fixture or promote successful-discovery timings to a no-author maximum. Seal validation, Authorship body/callback/wrapper and retained finalization need explicit non-duplicated owners; exact registered-prefix arithmetic does not prove their charge sufficiency. Native c=2 remains accepted without replay or rebinding.

**Source pins:** Selected local files and cached SDK sources below identify the inspected reduction, not a full build-input closure, distribution integrity, Wasm or executing-host attestation. SDK paths are under `~/.cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/`.

## Authorship Empty-Queue Coherence Countercase

**Question/result:** Does ordinary TrustedSet source preserve the roster premise needed to exclude no-author? Not generally. The narrow conditional theorem above remains valid, but its coherence premise cannot be inferred merely from ordinary mode or shared Session/Aura handling. Aura `change_authorities(empty)` explicitly returns without updating Authorities. Session can install an empty validator vector before invoking that handler. This is a source-backed transition countercase, not an executed valid block, public ancestry, exploit or price.

**Source profile:** Without `runtime-benchmarks`, `configs/staking_config.rs:53–63` returns TrustedSet unconditionally; LP fixture key/read is compiled only with that feature. Session's native scheduler is NeverEndSession; RuntimeSessionRotation supplies periodic rotation after Authorship. Named genesis supplies keys for the same invulnerable identities used by the manager. Its genesis calls for epochs0/1 read the same roster, and default QueuedChanged is false. Benchmark fixture writes and runtime upgrades are not premises of this countercase.

Let L be a nonempty initial invulnerable list, K its Aura keys, V the active Session validator vector, Q queued pairs and C QueuedChanged. Keep invulnerables unchanged and let every member sign the existing `Session::purge_keys` call. It removes NextKeys/key ownership and releases key custody references; it does not mutate V, Q or active Aura keys. Runtime BaseCallFilter does not deny that call. This source path requires authorized signer participation, not arbitrary deletion of another validator's keys.

| Transition | V / Aura | Q / C | Decisive source behavior |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Named genesis | L / K | pairs(L,K) / false | Manager genesis0/1 return the same keyed invulnerables |
| Signed purge of every NextKeys entry | L / K | unchanged / false | Purge affects future key loading, not already queued/current sets |
| First rotation | L / K | empty / true | Activate old Q; manager returns Some(L), all load_keys fail, next_changed starts true |
| Second rotation | empty / K | empty / true | Install empty Q into V; changed=true handler passes empty keys, Aura ignores the update |

**First-rotation distinction:** Initial C=false does not itself create this mismatch: genesis Q still activates the same validators/keys even after NextKeys is purged. The first rotation prepares empty Q with C=true because TrustedSet returns Some(L). The second rotation exposes the empty-update exception. Generic QueuedChanged=false/key-upgrade histories are not silently certified by this example.

**No-author consequence:** AuraExt finalization copies retained K, and its next validation guard can find an index/key from K; it does not read V. Cold Session-backed author lookup then sees V empty and returns None. Aura disabled checks read the disabled-index collection, not a nonempty V invariant. This identifies why seal/index validation alone cannot prove account discovery. No complete header/relay-proof/state-root replay was performed; remaining block/context checks and earlier cache/digest state retain their own obligations. The source transition falsifies broad coherence preservation without asserting that a particular full block has been admitted.

**Evidence-selected correction:** Preserve the accepted conditional entry-domain reduction and c=2 observations at their original scopes. Narrow its supporting roster argument: shared Session handling is insufficient at the empty update. Complete ordinary-domain Authorship qualification must retain None behavior or justify a stronger actually enforced admission premise; an unshipped nonempty-queue rule or assumed collator key health is not such a premise. No pricing or economic/consensus behavior change is authorized here.

**Next evidence boundary:** A default-native source-transition control should invoke actual signed-origin purge, two real reference rotation calls and cold Authorship lookup, asserting V/Q/Aura/callback/cache/finalization consequences without raw roster injection or a fabricated long chain. Such a control is not complete validation or a physical campaign. Do not repeat old c=0/1/2 or host controls merely for confidence; admit new execution only for this exposed transition. P28-19 remains the sole open-work owner.

**Identity:** The preceding12 source pins still identify the shared SDK/runtime implementations. Additional inspected source SHA-256: `template/runtime/src/configs/staking_config.rs` = `863975b110ca564fecf7dd99a3fd1f2d82b871d75c592c960e90a2111c3a2411`; `template/pallets/session-rotation/src/lib.rs` = `3ef9f60e155fceb2089c785f1ca7041d914da3a282ddea2ce305d623310a073d`. Decisive anchors: Session50 genesis552–579, purge725/989–1027, rotation769–859; Aura49 change235–250 and handler380–402; runtime manager676–689. These selected files are not a complete build/call-graph, distribution or executing-Wasm attestation.

**Independent disposition:** `authorship-empty-queue-source-review/0c2883bc-93e7-4633-97e2-ebba827d4496` returns **ACCEPT SOURCE TRANSITION ONLY**, no mandatory corrections. All three document pins, both additional source pins and seven preceding selected pins matched. Reviewer inspected decisive Session tuple forwarding, IdentityCollator mapping and empty-update behavior; SDK CollatorSelection31 matched Cargo.lock. One attempt, exit0, not killed, semantic acceptance accepted; stdout4804bytes SHA-256 `31d29b7885824e4279cf1ff69078f3144108aa17bb689ab1b83e624835d7567e`, execution SHA-256 `75260f84ed3f72b25dedd09f6e07aece064ddf1e04cd74effcf86db3d4f95f6b`, stderr empty. No native/build/physical validation was performed by the judge. One independent judge is not quorum.

**Disposition:** Source countercase/correction accepted and closed; default-native actual-call transition conformance is next. No new native/physical execution or code change occurred in this source cohort. Complete owner/method/host, physical bounds and production bindings remain unqualified.

## Authorship Empty-Queue Native Transition Control

**Question and carrier:** Does the accepted empty-queue source transition occur through actual reference calls, rather than raw roster writes? `runtime/src/tests/authorship.rs::authorship_empty_queue_transition_uses_actual_reference_calls` answers in default-native scope. Each declared preset uses the existing typed preset-genesis builder with empty code bytes for native execution only. Retained-key and purged-key peers both invoke RuntimeSessionRotation's adapter twice; no periodic blocks, sparse dates, history, raw validator/Aura/queue writes or FRAME-ledger injection are supplied.

**Preparation and authority:** A real signed-origin native transfer funds the pot. Missing-origin purge is rejected root-neutrally; each participating validator's own signed-origin RuntimeCall dispatch purges its NextKeys. This is pallet/dispatch authority conformance, not signature/nonce/fee-extension or ExternalPhase admission. First rotation keeps the active roster and sets QueuedChanged=true; second activates the prepared queue. The test checks both intermediate Session/Aura states and current indices. System initialization then supplies one component digest/number context; it is not Executive/Core/AuraExt validation or a descendant-block witness.

**Native result:** One passing test contains four cells, two declared presets crossed with retain/purge. Retained-key peers preserve the Session/Aura lists, discover the indexed account, pay the funded reward and register the existing callback declaration. Purged peers have empty Session validators/queue but unchanged nonempty Aura keys and a Some Aura index; cold account discovery returns None, pot/recipient/issuance and LastAuthoredBlock remain unchanged. Both initialize returns are zero. This distinguishes discovery None from c=0/1/2 recipient classes without adding a benchmark selector or price.

**Logical owner boundary:** Reuse the existing h1 observer only around actual single-pallet Executive registration and Authorship finalization. Both cells observe one cache Get, one cache Delete and net-zero cache keys; only the retained-key cell has cache Put. BlockWeight logical Put counts are2 with callback and1 without callback, even though the latter scalar remains zero. Verification's author/cache/ledger queries and observer snapshots are outside those phases. These are logical SDK operations, not DB I/O, CPU, proof-size increment, witness identity, a zero-cost certificate or a physical bound.

**Validation and identity:** Default-native focused test1/1 passes in `/tmp/deos-authorship-empty-queue-native.IoR420.log`, SHA-256 `32edf13d1efeea8da778024fde82cb7bdf9e4b6b1cfd515294cb4ae48d1b9754`. Format, default-runtime Clippy and workspace/all-target/all-feature Clippy `-D warnings` pass in `/tmp/deos-authorship-empty-queue-checks.fSnXCk.log`, SHA-256 `fd50cb3275b476c71995939e18e0a8e7e948c9f84fdc0b380bed7d54f532a8ba`. No code changed after these runs. The all-feature check excludes this default-only test; default execution/Clippy are the applicable evidence.

## Historical Generated Owner Inventory

The 2026-10-19 inventory was source parsing, not a fresh measured baseline. Released `v0.7.27` preserves its original coefficient/category tables and production-Wasm snapshot `5c1f797c…`. At that tag, `WeightInfo` has 119 methods and 118 generated bodies: 88 parameterless, 30 parameterized and one non-generated trait default. The later [compiled producer coordinate below](#generated-declaration-registry-and-native-acceptance) replaces category arithmetic; its population is distinct, not a new measurement of this historical source.

The retired category summary covered only 39 of 118 bodies, summed mutually exclusive owners, treated a largest per-owner proof as if it were combined, and excluded DB terms from RefTime. Selected declaration arithmetic uses RocksDbWeight at 25,000,000 ps/read and 100,000,000 ps/write; the compiled producer separates coefficients, read/write counts and runtime DB terms. `scripts/benchmarks.sh --check` rejects missing non-default methods and generated orphans. No sum, maximum of independent proof leaves or fee ratio supplies a whole-block or universal Close certificate. P28-08/19/20 retain actual caller, complete-bound and production-binding qualification.

## Deadline Quantum Source Classification

This read-only code/contract cohort closes the quantum-classification question exposed by the native W7 windows. Specification §§4/5.8/9 requires complete admission, bounded ordered sources, ordinary Service priority and Q1/B+1, not one Deadline member globally per block. One immutable Service round/one Step per Actor is a different invariant. The shipped one-attempt-per-clock lowering is coupled to a complete two-clock maximum and a uniform admitted-Service floor; increasing a loop bound without changing those owners is not an optimization.

| Source owner | Verified constraint |
| --- | --- |
| lib.rs::execute_mandatory_prepass | Freezes once, rejects domains below configured minima, reserves/settles one deadline_maximum, then dependency service and the ordinary Service round |
| lib.rs::service_due_deadline_frontiers | Prepays both branch maxima and invokes Block then Tick once; one clock cannot consume the other's mandatory authority |
| lib.rs::deadline_service_weight_upper | Adds selectors, max(return,review+Close) and max(temporal+entry/cleanup,financial due+cleanup); no single successful subtotal substitutes for D |
| lib.rs::mandatory_actor_control_envelopes | Shares P,D,Q+U,F between execution and Contract admission |
| lib.rs::ensure_contract_fits_service_budget | Preserves uniform G=L-(P+D+Q+U+F) for maximum Service Control; effect admission stays independent |
| configs/mod.rs::BlockResourceBudgetValue | Production retains configured budget; Native W7 uses no isolated-test override or settled-prefix credit |
| Resource policy §6.3 | Drain cannot reopen control stages; spare final-block Control is not pre-Service authority |

No O7 reopening, prototype, new sample or production change follows.

## Optional Quantum Peer-Fault Regression and Bootstrap Boundary

O43 source inspection exposed a concrete flaw in the existing O41 test-only model, not production: it normalized MemberMissing as absent even when a nonempty clock's heap page was missing. deadline_index_get reads the page/slot without consulting index length; absent and missing topology can therefore share that error. The new ordinary service_head_allowance_quantum_rejects_missing_nonempty_peer_index prepares an earlier real clock pair, then explicitly corrupts only Block index length while its root page is absent. Initial single-test default selection fails0PASS/1failed/exit101: Committed instead of RolledBack. That assertion fails before later preservation checks; RED alone does not certify their outcome or public reachability of the malformed state.

The existing model now captures bounded peer metadata before its worker and accepts MemberMissing only with declared zero length and absent root page. InvalidDestination and nonempty missing peers still refuse; no full namespace certificate, source scan, new enum or repair is added. Metadata reads are covered only by the model's nonzero outer token assumption, never a physical price. Green asserts exact pre-unit root, first source readiness retained/second source not latched, positive incurred Control and no outstanding reservation; only the injected length is then repaired before real ordinary Service. Source guards, actual two-clock worker and stored-prefix rollback remain project-owned.

Both canonical offline/release focused family selections pass6tests/0failed/0ignored, independently default and all-features, covering the original twelve contexts plus the new peer fault. The oracle binary reports0executed/13filtered and is excluded; neither execution modes nor model contexts are unique workload counts. These are changed-input regression runs, not reopening the closed O41 raw quote/timing collections. No new O43 prototype or loop is implemented. The actual-parent inspection finds a benchmark coverage defect before any O43 implementation. benchmarking.rs::scheduler_on_initialize_cutoff reads CurrentBlockResourceState, opens a synthetic configured budget, reserves/settles zero, reads fixture ActorReadyTail, writes PrepassExecutionCutoff, opens ExternalPhase and stores its state. Its verifier checks those synthetic outputs. It never calls actor_prepass or execute_mandatory_prepass. The generated method's retained storage comments likewise name ReadyTail/Cutoff and a historical75-byte resource-state ceiling. Its current declared [259498000ps,1560bytes] includes two25m reads/two100m writes; these are booked inputs, not a physical bound for different code.

Production uses no ReadyTail/Cutoff in this path. scheduler.rs ignores the supplied cutoff argument and runs canonical Service. The ordinary Service child owns its own discovery/turn work, so the table does not infer that every operation belongs to P or double-charge children. The defect is absent source/applicability coverage for the coordination parent, not demonstrated numerical undercoverage, a larger physical proof or permission to subtract child timings.

The public inherent contract rejects missing/before-context/duplicate sequencing at block validity; check_inherent itself validates payload/version, not canonical-state ordering. BadOrigin, incomplete context, duplicate, malformed/future resource state and invalid-budget/clock hosts are diagnostic/refusal classes, not silently priced as accepted reference execution. Parent errors after child mutation roll back the public call; per-clock worker outcomes may instead return inside Ok, so not every child refusal invalidates the parent. Successful on_finalize takes/removes CurrentBlockResourceState (lib.rs::Hooks::on_finalize); on_initialize is a no-op. Absent state is therefore the canonical next-Prepass source, and c0 matches that one geometry. Injected previous/future values are diagnostic, not ordinary lineage: the duplicate predicate tests equality only, so unequal values fall through to fresh construction subject to subsequent guards. No new stale/future rejection follows.

Native cfg(test) for_prepass also has an explicitly isolated override; emitted benchmark builds use the trait default. Prepared context helper writes synthetic ValidationData; production Timestamp/ValidationData input lineage still needs transfer proof. Canonical resource-state absence follows successful finalization, not a carried previous-block value.

Proposed smallest engineering slice for review: extract only the existing straight-line prefix from context_ready through the initial P settlement into one private production-used begin_mandatory_prepass helper, returning its existing state/budget/tick/four envelopes. Keep the public origin/block lookup/FRAME layer, every branch, child call and final publication in the existing caller. ## Production-Used Entry Source Isolation

Installed begin_mandatory_prepass and its fixed crate-private PrepassEntry return, consumed by the real public-parent implementation and extra/raw-only mandatory_prepass_entry_control(c). The literal context-through-initial-P-settlement prefix and the complete downstream source suffix compare equal to HEAD's original execute_mandatory_prepass; only return-record construction and parent destructuring/call wiring are new. Both envelope derivations, callback ordering/counts, equality-only duplicate predicate, duplicate write-before-Err, public origin/FRAME transaction/return and every downstream branch remain. A settled reservation has no destructor; moving its local drop to helper return does not introduce custody or reservation effects. This is source equivalence, not timing equivalence.

The entry control measures the shared helper with an explicit with_storage_layer/Error-to-DispatchError adapter. c0 returns memory-local PrepassExecuting, valid frozen budget/tick/envelopes, settled P and zero outstanding/effect use without publishing state. c1 first calls the successful real public parent, then checks exact duplicate refusal and unchanged committed state/root. The adapter excludes public origin/block lookup/return glue, breaker, children and final publication; it is not the whole public dispatchable or complete P. The native real-SDK checker is shared with the whole-parent control, preserving extra-only metadata, positive canonical key access, verifier-only legacy reads, saved-proof separation, increased verifier DB work and parent restoration.

## Deadline Coordination Family Proposal

Source-only family, frozen before further extraction or collection. Current lib84744997/benchmarkinge11e2ae7/scheduler3e2e8c8a. No source/native/timing campaign is replayed for confidence and no new kernel helper is installed by this proposal. The ownership question is the actual normal Prepass Deadline reserve/meter/call/settle region, not another optional quantum.

Sibling inspection finds existing complete-pair controls: scheduler_due_deadline_to_service and scheduler_due_deadline_to_service_deep_index call service_due_deadline_frontiers inside the recorded block, including its full-envelope calculation/admission and Block-then-Tick dispatch. Their Block retry geometry is populated-page removal versus a singleton minimum removed from the full admitted index. Their meter is Weight::MAX, now_tick=0 and next_review=None; the peer Tick may process host-genesis work. Thus the shared pair is not missing, but these bodies exclude parent ledger reserve/settle and do not use the normal caller's finite meter/current tick/B+1-review inputs.

The active Prepass D reservation is deadline_service_weight_upper: independent selectors plus component-wise branch maxima, then settlement by the atomic meter's consumed Weight. It does not call the two scheduler_due_* Weight getters. Those getters remain max operands in scheduler_complete_outer_weight_upper and are explicitly retained conservative Service selector entries in the runtime budget test. A max operand is not a second additive Deadline charge; deleting it would be a separate floor/binding change, not this ownership repair. Keep both existing pair methods and their historical captures unchanged.

| Family cell | Bounded source contract |
| --- | --- |
| Existing source reuse | Share/reuse the two existing retry fixture producers; do not copy another parent or invent a quiet reference population. Prepared fixture ancestry, not admitted production history |
| Proposed production boundary | A crate-private service_prepass_deadlines using existing now/tick/state/budget/D arguments, containing only the current normal parent region from reserve through settle; return the current consumed Weight, preserving dropped per-clock outcomes and exact errors |
| Explicit consumer | One extra/raw-only mandatory_prepass_deadline_control with c0 populated-page retry and c1 deep-index retry, coupled to the actual caller if extraction is selected. No one-use helper, driver, new storage or WeightInfo port |
| Setup | Existing retry preparation and real first Attempt, existing neighbor/deep-index construction, required host context, then actual begin_mandatory_prepass. Keep all reference activity; no forced cleared Tick clock, fabricated funding or authority |
| Recorded caller | Exact wrapper inside the public-equivalent storage-layer/error adapter, using the entry's frozen finite D maximum and actual now_tick plus checked B+1 review key. Prefix/context setup and verifier stay outside body |
| Output checks | Intended Block retry returns once with next-block eligibility, Run/paid prefix retained; local phase remains PrepassExecuting, local Effect usage unchanged, no outstanding reservation and Control delta equals returned atomic-meter consumption on both axes |
| Tick classification | Actual current-preset peer work remains included but typed Tick outcome is not certified by the wrapper's Weight-only return. Do not infer quiet/healthy absence from success or errors, add a receipt solely for measurement, or claim all Tick branches covered |
| Native SDK | Both categorical endpoints and verify-off/on; fresh parents and saved proof before verification. Require actual Deadline body-key evidence, not copied assumptions that canonical resource state must be read inside a local-ledger wrapper |
| Source refusal | Unconstructible source, bad context/budget, unintended Block head, mismatch in selected Block geometry/ledger/result or invalid input clock is a stopped source cell, not a quiet success; do not add cells or weaken checks after failure |

For the selected Block Retry, next_review is not used by process_next_due_block_deadline's Retry branch; replacing None by checked B+1 alone has an explicit noninterference argument there. Changing now_tick is not similarly inert: it may change peer eligibility and effects. The native source control therefore needs to verify the actual selected Block source and reconcile total consumed Control, including any peer work; it cannot inherit prior raw timings/proofs or assume silent Tick. The Weight-only wrapper does not expose a per-clock receipt, so richer Tick-result qualification is deliberately excluded rather than adding measurement-only production output. The inner service_due_deadline_frontiers retains its additional deadline-envelope derivation after the prefix's two derivations; do not cache or consolidate queries.

Current ownership stays P-owned fixed orchestration plus D atomic selectors/workers. A future inclusive whole-wrapper D replacement would have to reserve/settle a qualified complete owner and retire the contained global atomic additions for that invocation; atom weights may still serve inner admission and independent callers. P must then stop owning the same glue. Merely appending a wrapper coefficient to P/D, subtracting helper timings, or removing conservative Service max operands is rejected. This source-only wrapper proposal keeps current accounting and therefore does not itself implement that future inclusive binding.

The two retry profiles are not a full D maximum family. Block reviews (wake/rearm/close), temporal Idle/busy outcomes, qualified financial-wait return/close, performed rollback/refusal paths, topology bounds and matching original/overlay lineage remain separate qualification obligations. Default versus benchmark optional financial supplements remain a real path difference. No new numeric model, emitted samples, production price, expanded Service floor, fee, quantum or O43 implementation follows. The review disposition below validates this bounded source proposal, not native constructibility or pricing; BACKLOG alone owns subsequent work.

## Deadline Family Review Disposition

Run deadline-coordination-family-review completed with original source reviewer005 and ownership reviewer006 both APPROVE and source verifier007 agreeing: the proposed contiguous normal-parent reserve-through-settle boundary and two retry-source consumers are constructible in the inspected source. No required proposal correction was found. Coordinator read originals005..009 through the recorded execution.json capture paths. Merger/judge/normalizer receive summaries by reference and declare degraded fidelity; preserve that scope instead of claiming an independently complete synthesis. These are source reviews, not executed native or physical qualification.

Verified constraints: the shared pair already has two controls; its Weight::MAX/tick0/None signature does not include parent finite reservation/settlement. Retry ignores next_review, whereas changing now_tick may change the peer work. The pair returns Ok containing each clock result, so a Weight-only wrapper cannot certify Tick health. BlockResourceState::reserve/settle mutate local memory, which a storage-layer rollback does not rewind. Successful source-cell phase/Control/outstanding assertions therefore apply only after successful wrapper completion; stopped/error cells retain their exact semantics and are not repaired or given universal rollback claims.

Both reviewers inspected the actual lib84744997, benchmarkinge11e2ae7 and scheduler3e2e8c8a source. Their source-backed minimality is relative to the selected parent ledger boundary, not a global refactor necessity theorem. Existing pair methods and conservative Service max operands stay unchanged. A future inclusive D still requires retiring contained global atomic additions and duplicated P glue, with no present numeric/binding/G/fee change. No extra per-clock receipt, copied parent, new driver or measurement-only production output is justified.

Acceptance of an implementation requires the real parent and extra/raw-only control to share the literal existing region, established fixture reuse, actual clock/B+1/finite D inputs, selected Block recovery and paid Run checks, successful local-ledger reconciliation, and native SDK verify/body-key evidence. Finite-budget endpoint construction, actual peer behavior and proof separation remain unexecuted. Complete D maxima/branch coverage, default-host/lineage transfer, physical RefTime/ProofSize, fees and release readiness remain unqualified. BACKLOG owns that implementation cohort; no unchanged green corpus is replayed by this review disposition.

## Dependency Coordination Family Proposal

Source audit: lib.rs::execute_mandatory_prepass already owns reserve→finite meter→dependency_scan_source_probe→optional process_next_dependency_scan_unit→consumed→settle. Q is the probe declaration; U is component-wise max(member, completion). Its envelope is Q+U. Empty selector consumes Q and releases U; every positive probe consumes Q+U even for completion. The worker charges U before its storage transaction and rolls back failed source/registration/Pending mutations, not its meter. Probe or worker errors become ResourceProtocolFailed before settlement/Service/publication. No free probe, actual-branch discount, extra unit, callback caching, fee or new physical owner follows.

Existing controls already record the actual probe and worker, not missing machinery. Positive dependency_scan_source_probe seeds only cursor/count, so is not a whole-worker topology producer. process_dependency_scan_unit resolves a balance source, publicly creates a Mutable System Actor with parked-balance activation, publishes a revision and records the real worker. process_dependency_scan_completion_unit deliberately seeds a standalone zero-length revision/source carrier without public balance registration; it proves that prepared primitive point, not registered Actor history. All use Weight::MAX and omit parent ledger reservation/settlement. Existing wide Close/temporal helpers also prepare real member/handoff histories; their broad populations are not required for this minimal wrapper family.

Proposed minimal service_prepass_dependency_scan(state, budget, envelope) returns only the existing consumed Weight. It shares the literal original parent block, with no new checks, receipts, scheduler entrypoint or typed outcome. Proposed mandatory_prepass_dependency_control(c) is exact/extra/raw-only and has three successful native source classes, not a price or maximum:

| c | Source preparation / required predicate | Successful verifier / booked Control delta |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Actual healthy empty selector after real prefix and Deadline prelude; fail closed if unavailable, never clear or drain reference sources to manufacture it | Empty selector retained, no worker-owned source change; Q |
| 1 | Reuse the existing real member setup producer; authoritative selector must be the unmodified singleton for the new source, fixed target has one live registration and no destination yet | Exact actor/source/revision Pending event, registration acknowledged and cursor advanced once; Q+U |
| 2 | Same real producer, exactly one real member unit in setup advances to cursor=end with revision=target; no synthetic completion seed or forced cursor | Existing event/registration retained, target cleared, source removed and selector empty through actual worker completion; Q+U |

Extract only that existing member setup producer for a second consumer; preserve all existing recorded bodies/verifier tails and completion/probe fixtures. New source classes require canonical topology/try_state checks and explicit supported-host predicates. Preparation is not an authenticated/admitted block history and must not claim two units in one legal Prepass. Failure to select the intended source or to construct exact completion is BenchmarkError::Stop, not permission to replace the head, clear peers, add adaptive work or invent chronology. Real begin_mandatory_prepass and service_prepass_deadlines run outside the recorded block before the new boundary; snapshot after that prelude, preserving any real preceding mutations. Peer Deadline outcomes stay opaque.

Successful cells reconcile local phase/budget, unchanged effect use, no outstanding reservation, unpublished canonical resource state and exact Q versus Q+U delta. Source/Actor semantic invariants and untouched unrelated state are verified outside recording; selected singleton removal must produce the exact empty selector. Refusal is a separate diagnostic/native obligation: malformed selector or admitted worker fault must remain ResourceProtocolFailed, public FRAME rollback must preserve its storage prefix, and private borrowed-ledger error cells must not claim memory restoration or settlement. No corrupt-source raw sample or production-reachable history is inferred. Existing carrier/member rollback tests are not themselves public-parent/local-ledger proof.

Native SDK plan: c0..2 × two real parent contexts × verify±, correct shared metadata oracle using DependencyScanSourceListState namespace evidence, identical saved body proof and positive verifier-read separation; do not interpret times/root fields. Shapes are finite success controls only. Empty/member/completion omission, callback/order changes, ledger deltas, failed metadata oracle or constructibility refusal are falsifiers, not occasions for coefficient/margin repairs. Full stale/reclamation/handoff/destination/max-geometry domain, error qualification, default/benchmark transfer, original/overlay lineage, complete P/D and shared Service/tail remain excluded. Existing Q and U declarations stay; any future inclusive replacement must retire contained additions and duplicate glue. No extraction or collection is performed by this proposal.

Inspected source identities retain Deadline kernel82d998bd, benchmarkingf96545a2 and runtime SDK oracleabd57705; these are proposal references, not new timing/artifact identities. Source anchors: lib.rs::dependency_scan_source_probe/process_next_dependency_scan_unit/complete_dependency_scan; benchmarking.rs::dependency_scan_source_probe/process_dependency_scan_unit/process_dependency_scan_completion_unit/prepare_wide_idle_scan_handoff; tests/storage_and_api.rs::dependency_scan_source_carrier_preserves_exact_fair_membership_and_rollback/dependency_scan_processes_one_destination_before_advancing; runtime tests::actor_control_uses_exact_one_third_of_schedulable_weight. No native tests or physical corpus are replayed for this source-only audit.

## Normal Tail and Shared Service Ownership

Source-only audit at kernel f68970a8, scheduler3e2e8c8a, benchmarkingd09fbf93 and resource types’ existing phase/reservation contract. Resource-policy §§2/6.2/6.3 owns the frozen common ceiling, independent domains and one-way phase order. No code extraction, native replay, new sample or coefficient follows. The current public mandatory_prepass_control(c1) already records the complete normal tail through the real dispatchable. This is source inclusion, not proof of a missing benchmark, maximum geometry or a separate constant P charge.

| Boundary | Actual owner/source | Existing evidence and non-overlap limit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Remaining Control, F subtraction and combined limit | lib.rs::execute_mandatory_prepass | Frozen budget minus current booked Control minus retained scheduler_on_idle_base+block_resource_finalize; then add Actor base turn. P glue, already inside actual public-parent control |
| Pass entry/reservation | scheduler.rs::execute_cycle_to_cutoff_inner(Some) | Zero-limit exit precedes reserve. Otherwise reserve one pass-wide Control maximum; failure halts optional work and returns zero consumed. Not another per-Actor Control debit |
| Encounter loop | service_canonical_round_head_with_reserved_control | Independent remaining Control, actual effect delta and bounded executed/scanned counters; shared Prepass/Drain work, not constant P |
| Pass exit/settlement | CyclePass::reconciled_domains and enclosing loop | Clean actual Control is consumed−effects. Uncertain reconciliation retains Control maximum and halts; failed actual settlement retries maximum. Clean algebra is not the error-cell contract |
| External publication/return | execute_mandatory_prepass normal suffix | Open External or halt, publish CurrentBlockResourceState and return P+D_actual+dependency_actual+pass.consumed. Child work is included, not a second owner |
| Later Drain/finalization | Hooks::on_idle/on_finalize | F is withheld from Prepass and accounted in idle housekeeping; Drain has a new pass Control reservation/effect domain and finishes Finalizable+telemetry. Finalize takes/verifies state; no source-only idle-headroom theorem follows |

For clean reconciled Prepass only, write H=P+D_actual+dependency_actual, C_s=pass.consumed−pass.effect_consumed, E_s=pass.effect_consumed, C=frozen Control, B=frozen Actor base turn and F=idle-base+finalize. The tail supplies Control C−H−F and combined limit C−H−F+B. Its returned actual is H+C_s+E_s; F is not charged again there. This is declared/booked algebra, not physical timing, witness subtraction or a new uniform G. Uncertain effects/max-Control fallback are excluded from that clean equality; a successful whole-parent control verifies only its selected healthy cells.

Existing complete callers have distinct seams. scheduler_service_successful_interior and retry profiles invoke execute_cycle→execute_cycle_to_cutoff_inner(None): real loop/head work, but no pass resource reservation, independent Control ceiling or effect-delta reconciliation. Existing User inspection/shared/no-Action controls invoke service_canonical_round_head_with_resources: real per-call resource booking, but no enclosing pass reservation/loop. The production Some path instead calls with_reserved_control, suppressing nested Control booking while retaining full actual reporting. The actual public-parent control includes that path; package direct/pass-owned conformance and runtime saturated_prepass_preserves_drain_finalization_reachability support selected native behavior, not complete physical prices. The latter supplies Weight::MAX to on_idle and does not establish actual Executive idle headroom. Original tests were inspected, not replayed.

The current benchmarking module has no direct execute_cycle_to_cutoff_with_resources call; that is a missing caller-isolation consumer, not missing Service machinery or proof that the public-parent control omits it. No new tail helper is justified: the resource-aware pass is already a production-used function and direct resource-aware fixtures already exist. A finite source bridge can use those owners rather than copy the parent, invent a scheduler or label helper timing difference as credit. Full-parent/loop/direct-encounter profiles are non-additive; any inclusive owner must retire contained charges before production selection.

Idle housekeeping first reserves available Control, settles F and publishes state; the later pass reserves the remaining Control. Those sequential maxima are not two identical debits. F is a separate source-transfer gap, not repaired by this audit. scheduler_on_idle_base records a synthetic reserve/zero settle, current-state put, breaker plus retired readiness reads and starvation update. block_resource_finalize prepares finish_drain outside recording, then records take/snapshot put/verification. Actual on_idle performs current-state/budget checks, phase transitions, pass execution, finish_drain, snapshot/publication and conditional starvation; actual on_finalize later takes/verifies the stored state/snapshot. Those recorded fragments are not literal complete wrapper coverage, even though some operations coincide. No numerical deficit, free bookkeeping, smaller F, overlap credit or unconditional hook availability is inferred. The None-state idle fallback cannot serve as valid complete-block history: the real finalizer rejects absent/non-Finalizable state.

The next narrowed decision is a finite actual resource-aware pass source bridge using existing canonical User/no-Action or equivalent supported construction, with explicit phase/domain, finite combined/Control inputs, zero/positive progress, refusal and reconciliation boundaries before collection. Constructor availability, exact selected frontier and original/overlay/host transfer remain unverified. Pricing retirement, F/whole-runtime qualification and release artifacts remain with their existing backlog owners. This dossier owns source findings and exclusions, not a second queue or an optimization result.

## Finite Resource-Aware Pass Bridge Proposal

Proposed scheduler_resource_pass_control(c0..3) is one extra/raw-only consumer of the existing execute_cycle_to_cutoff_with_resources; no production extraction, new driver, state, receipt, fee, WeightInfo or coefficient. Reuse prepare_canonical_user_opening_no_action(0) unchanged: publicly prefunded User, real paid readiness, full-header/maximum-Step source, unbegun singleton round, exact Pipeline-only custody and a preconstructed FreshDrain local ledger. Preserve its B1→eligible-block setup; add no clock advance, source aging or owner mutation. This is configured-budget prepared storage, not a real settled-prefix freeze, authenticated/admitted Drain history or actual FRAME remainder. Prepared-round and broader phase/effect domains remain excluded from this first bridge; duplicating existing direct profiles is not a prerequisite.

All cells retain FreshDrain/ActorDrainEffect, exact generation/frontier, zero prior resource usage, no outstanding reservation/halt and canonical source. Let C=source limits.actor_control, E=limits.shared_economic and I=the existing fixture.inspection (populated begin+eligible probe+state probe). Require positive I in both axes, I≤C and checked finite C+E. Use only caller-owned local state and the unchanged production pass; do not call the direct head first, fake its encounter result, modify the queue or transfer control/effect capacity. For all nonzero cells keep the same finite combined C+E while varying only the pass Control cap; caps may be stricter than the available configured domain. This isolates independent Control admission rather than simultaneously exhausting the combined meter. These diagnostic inputs are not a claim about a particular parent’s actual FRAME remainder. Each unsupported predicate stops rather than widening limits. No hook/base/finalize work or phase transition is inside this selected function call.

| c | Finite pass inputs | Expected selected boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Combined C+E, pass Control C | Actual no-Action Opening; one Pipeline debit/collector credit, retained hold and typed ServiceFee funding refusal, Suspended Run cursor0, zero invoked Task/effect. Positive service-control progress, not an S/A/economic-throughput claim |
| 1 | Combined0, pass Control C | Existing early zero-input return before reservation/selector access: consumed/effect0, unchanged Rust ledger and storage/source, no halt/starvation observation. Zero booked Weight is not zero CPU or a free-check price |
| 2 | Combined C+E; Control=(I.RefTime,C.ProofSize) | Inspection fits; complete Control fails RefTime despite other combined/effect capacity. Actor/round/custody/holds/root unchanged, consumed and local Control delta I, zero effect, ordinary starvation/stop, no outstanding reservation/halt |
| 3 | Combined C+E; pass Control=(C.RefTime,I.ProofSize) | Independent ProofSize analogue of c2; same source and preserved rights, no cross-axis/economic borrowing |

Success verifiers must check CyclePass’s actual reconciled pair, ledger delta/budget/phase, zero effects and complete reservations. Do not manufacture ServiceRoundEncounter::Eligible to reuse a direct-head verifier or fill the unused fixture meter with a synthetic value. Reuse semantic assertion code only through an earned common outcome helper, or explicitly check the actual Run, exact fees/holds and canonical residence in the new footer. c0 may include a concluding empty/closed probe depending on caps; no exact helper-cost difference or scalar candidate price follows. Refusal source must fit I within both finite inputs and demonstrate the intended independent Control boundary, not substitute an earlier discovery/combined-meter failure.

Native plan: explicit all four endpoints × verify±, existing constructor consumer regression if affected, and the existing SDK host over both real parents. Extend the shared source checker minimally rather than a second harness: c0/2/3 require recorded ServiceHeader namespace evidence; c1 must not inherit a positive-key oracle because the zero-input body never reads it. Saved-proof equality and positive verifier-read separation remain required, but no zero-proof, time/root-cost or backend-wide zero-I/O inference. Legacy absence checks stay verification-only; native verifier may read exact state outside recording. Source-only review must distinguish oracle/method defects from unexecuted constructibility. No new physical recording, pricing or inclusive retirement is admitted by this proposal.

Relevant inspected owners: benchmarking.rs::prepare_user_pipeline_capacity/prepare_canonical_user_inspection_refusal/prepare_canonical_user_opening_no_action/assert_canonical_user_opening_no_action; scheduler.rs::execute_cycle_to_cutoff_inner/CyclePass::reconciled_domains; lib.rs::service_canonical_round_head_inner; types/resource.rs::reserve/settle/phase methods. Existing scheduling tests cover per-axis independent pass refusal/recovery, including separate Prepass/Drain domains; they were inspected, not replayed or promoted into this new SDK caller. Current kernel f68970a8/scheduler3e2e8c8a/benchmarkingd09fbf93/runtime SDKfd87f8c8 are unchanged. Source bridge implementation and its four native cells remain unexecuted; no quantitative best-result or Experiment status changes.

## Coordination Ownership Decision and Collection Gates

Selected source/accounting baseline: preserve the existing granular Control/effect partition, not an inclusive whole-parent P. This is a qualification-boundary decision, not acceptance of carried numerical prices or a new physical architecture. The source/native cohorts close call-path conformance only. No further wrapper, coefficient, floor credit, optional O43 work or collection is admitted by that closure.

| Exclusive target region | Qualification owner / exclusion |
| --- | --- |
| Fixed Prepass orchestration | P: pallet-public wrapper, entry/context/freeze checks, both envelope derivations, outer D/X reserve/meter/settle, remaining-limit arithmetic, Prepass invocation’s fixed pass shell and final publication/return. Exclude child bodies, repeated Service work, effects and external FRAME/fixed accounting |
| Two-clock Deadline work | D: actual frontier helper’s internal complete-bound derivation/check, Block/Tick selectors and selected worker branches. Keep independent clocks and existing branch maxima; outer Prepass ledger glue belongs P |
| Dependency source work | X=Q+max(member,completion): probe and at most one worker. Outer parent ledger glue belongs P; no branch discount or second source unit |
| Repeated Service work | Per-encounter Control includes complete admitted discovery/head/inner control and repeated loop bookkeeping, including empty/closed/refused encounters. Not constant P/F merely because bookkeeping lies outside the head function |
| Actor effects | Canonical effect provider/settlement; retain current StopCycle-to-Control mapping and actual incurred-effect rollback. Never infer economic success or an effect subtraction from a control-only source |
| Fixed Drain/finalization orchestration | F: actual idle wrapper/phase/telemetry/finalizer and that Drain invocation’s fixed pass shell; exclude repeated Service work and effects. The same shell source executes under different callers, not two charges for one invocation |

P/F ownership of fixed pass-shell work is the selected qualification target, not a claim that legacy declarations already cover its physical cost. Inspection/refusal and per-Actor fee eligibility remain separate from global resource ownership: derive_pipeline_machine_envelope already consumes service_turn_control_weight_upper, while pre-Opening/uncommitted refusal expense has its independent contract. Any owner refresh must reconcile quotes/certificates/fees without adding a fifth fee or charging paid Pipeline rights twice. mandatory_actor_control_weight_upper and contract service admission consume the same mandatory terms; preserve protected G, both axes, block ceiling and 1:2 allocation rather than repairing an underbound by shrinking a floor.

| Installed inclusive source control | Retirement required before any direct production use |
| --- | --- |
| Actual public parent | Includes P, actual D/X, Service Control and effects; F’s retained arithmetic is not its later execution. Reject direct constant-P promotion. A future inclusive model needs explicit domain separation and contained-owner retirement, not another additive global charge |
| Deadline wrapper | Contains P’s outer D glue plus D. Retire contained global atomic D addition and reissue P’s residual source if selecting an inclusive D owner. Keep scheduler_due_* operands in the conservative Service maximum; max membership is not a second additive Deadline debit |
| Dependency wrapper | Contains P’s outer X glue plus Q/U. Inclusive replacement must remove those contained global additions and requalify residual P; internal admission/meter ingredients may remain without another global charge |
| Resource-aware pass | Contains caller shell, repeated head/loop Control and effects. Do not add it beside I/K/common/Task owners or label it fixed P/F. A future complete owner must separate Control/effects and retain only source-disjoint residuals |

No old-P minus helper-time arithmetic is an admissible residual owner. Existing composite controls support conformance, counterexamples and future bounded methods; a native root/returned-Weight equality does not determine CPU, original witness scope, maximum geometry or safe retirement. A coefficient change would invalidate the consumed admissions/certificates/fee/artifact identities and requires their coherent existing release lane, not a shortcut in this source decision.

At this ownership decision's earlier source snapshot, two concrete transfer gates remained. First, actor_config.rs bound RuntimeAdmissionCertificateAuthority in ordinary builds but BenchmarkAdmissionCertificateAuthority under runtime-benchmarks. The former implements current() and inherits absent optional supplements; the latter supplies fixture-only financial allowances. Entry/D bound derivations can therefore reach extra current()/configured-bounds/lifecycle work under the benchmark host. Source superset wording is neither physical domination nor default-host qualification. Both native SDK parents still use that one compiled benchmark authority.

Second, frame-benchmarking50::v1.rs’s on_before_start commits externalities after setup and before recording, preloads whitelisted keys into the recorder and resets DB counters. Its post-body commit/root phases are separately ordered. In partial controls, an outside-recording entry/Deadline prelude is therefore committed into the measured starting backend; inside the real whole-parent body, earlier phases can leave overlay state for later children. Same values, source expressions or booked Weight do not establish identical original/overlay lineage, recorder baseline or repair inputs. Apply the global State Witness Accounting contract, not full-key conclusions from SDK32-byte metadata or automatic co-read credits.

At that earlier snapshot, scripts/benchmarks.sh --prepare/--check used fixed cargo features runtime-benchmarks, and runtime Cargo had no separate authority-fixture feature. The alias has only ordinary/benchmark branches. A minimal next profile design can investigate making optional authority scaffolding explicit while a plain benchmark lane retains the ordinary provider; preserve existing all-feature fixture semantics, fail closed for unsupported optional fixtures, bind exact feature/artifact identities and do not claim all other benchmark/default differences disappear. No feature, default, script contract or provider was changed by this decision.

Collection remains Not Prepared for a complete production owner. A supported pinned SDK collector exists and local recording is authorized; permission is not the blocker. Before a new run, a named consumer must bind exact source/feature/host/collector, fresh artifact, finite geometry/branch domain, start root/recorder/input lineage, inclusion rule/statistic and smallest falsifier. A default-authority mismatch or original/overlay rebinding fails transfer; wrong combined/independent domain or retained contained charge fails composition. Unsupported upper-bound inference stays gated rather than creating extra native campaigns, arbitrary margins, new physical wrappers or speculative caps. ## Ordinary Authority Lane Proposal

Proposed smallest baseline is an explicit preparation/native-conformance lane, not a new measurement or pricing system. One runtime-only Cargo feature benchmark-authority-scaffolding implies runtime-benchmarks and selects the existing BenchmarkAdmissionCertificateAuthority; without it, select the existing RuntimeAdmissionCertificateAuthority, including in a runtime-benchmarks build. No new provider/trait, runtime storage, on-chain selector, consensus event, economic policy or ordinary production behavior. Bare Cargo --features runtime-benchmarks changes from implicit fixture authority to ordinary authority; that change must be explicit in review/docs, not silently relabeled as historical behavior.

| Build coordinate | Selected authority / preserved boundary |
| --- | --- |
| Ordinary default/release | Existing Runtime authority, no optional fixture supplements; unchanged |
| runtime-benchmarks only | Existing Runtime authority; new ordinary-authority benchmark/native lane, not full production-Wasm equivalence |
| runtime-benchmarks plus benchmark-authority-scaffolding | Existing Benchmark authority and fixture allowances; preserve old fixture behavior |
| --all-features | Includes scaffolding feature, so preserves the existing benchmark fixture authority and Some-required semantics |

Repository script compatibility is deliberate: propose --authority-profile fixtures|ordinary, default fixtures. Default --check/--prepare/build/fallback must explicitly pass the scaffolding feature and keep the existing target/benchmarks path, preserving their old authority semantics rather than relying on bare runtime-benchmarks. Ordinary uses only runtime-benchmarks and a separate target/benchmarks-ordinary-authority path. Source/artifact identities change with the new feature topology even when fixture behavior stays equivalent; old captures remain tied to their old source/feature/artifact, not rebound to the new profile name.

Progressive baseline: ordinary profile initially supports only --check and standalone --prepare with that named modifier. Reject ordinary --skip-build, --runtime, raw collection, generation, merge, Cargo fallback and unrelated action/measurement arguments before prerequisites/build/bencher effects. Fixture defaults and archived exact/raw reuse keep their existing semantics without any ordinary-provider assertion. Ordinary must resolve only its own built path, never the fixture cache, production artifact or an external override. This avoids a new sidecar/marker/authority-receipt framework; reusable profile attestation can be earned later by an actual collection consumer. A requested profile, target path, exit0 or old hash alone is not proof of fresh/current-source Wasm or its executed authority. --prepare must report requested profile, exact Cargo features/path/hash as preparation identity only; inspect actual wbuild feature inputs and retain freshness/source closure before any later collection.

Native precision must remain project-owned. At proposal freeze, ci-local.sh supported only auto/all/default feature modes. Propose minimal --features LIST mode for Cargo-backed checks/tests, mutually exclusive with --all-features/--default-features and rejected for format or conflicting repeats. Retain Cargo defaults; validate a nonempty restricted feature-list grammar and shell-quote it, with isolated self-tests proving argv fidelity and rejection before execution. This enables the existing runner’s deos-runtime source checks under runtime-benchmarks alone; do not bypass it with a raw command inventory or add another runner. Existing all/default defaults and test-filter/nonempty/timeout contracts remain unchanged.

Planned focused native proof: test the associated authority type and optional supplement presence through the runtime binding under ordinary, ordinary-benchmark and all-feature fixture coordinates, without a production introspection hook. Reuse existing both-parent source SDK controls in the ordinary lane; explicitly separate cfg(test) budget/helper behavior from a production claim. Preserve all-feature fixture assertions and use input-equivalence rather than replaying unchanged campaigns. Some-required optional fixtures remain unavailable/fail closed under the ordinary provider; do not force fake allowances, globally skip failing assertions or market that lane as complete all-capability acceptance. The new scope removes only the named authority mismatch, not every benchmark/test/default difference, original/overlay transfer, maxima or actual FRAME budget gate.

Root script self-test covers both profiles’ feature/target routing, ordinary forbidden reuse/measurement/merge combinations, preparation modifier exclusivity, no bencher/sample/weight side effect in prepare, production-artifact protection and no cross-profile fallback. Native checks use SKIP_WASM_BUILD=1; fresh preparation requires it absent. No fresh Wasm or capture is required to accept the initial code/route contract. A later collection extension must separately bind current source, feature closure, actual artifact/profile, host/collector, legal finite domain and recorder/input lineage; do not open the route just because native source controls pass.

This selects one explicit fixture feature plus two narrow existing-entrypoint extensions over two priority-interacting authority features, arbitrary cfg/RUSTFLAGS/environment selectors, duplicated runtimes, trait overrides or generic evidence layers. Current source controls and protocol invariants stay untouched. Inspection anchors: runtime/Cargo.toml features, actor_config.rs authority definitions/Config alias, benchmarks.sh::build_benchmarks/prepare_benchmark_runtime/resolve_runtime_wasm_path/check_only/main, and ci-local.sh feature-mode/parser/help contract. At proposal freeze: no code, feature, default or artifact changed, no native run or collection performed.

## Prepass Prefix Lineage Source Map

Consumer is Actors/P28-08/Prepass Coordination. This source audit narrows the SDK-to-parent transfer gate; it is not measurement, a new Experiment or a complete transfer method. The State Witness Accounting contract remains authoritative. Reference-host source facts do not certify another host's trait implementations or extend frozen c0 to children.

| Source cut | Persistent state / recorder distinction |
| --- | --- |
| Successful fresh entry | begin_mandatory_prepass returns local state/budget/tick/envelopes; successful reserve/settle does not publish CurrentBlockResourceState. Concrete DEOS context checks and clock access read Timestamp/ValidationData; configured budget and Weight arithmetic do not write storage. Entry reads may already occupy the parent recorder. The existing fresh-entry control checks unchanged root, not identical retained recorder input |
| Entry refusal/publication | Duplicate entry writes a halted existing resource state before Err, but the public FRAME layer and entry control adapter roll that write back. Failed begin_prepass may publish before Err. Frozen/normal success publishes in execute_mandatory_prepass, outside successful entry. Do not model these as the fresh successful cut |
| Deadline to dependency | Dependency control executes entry and actual two-clock Deadline setup before SDK recording. Any committed Deadline changes become its starting backend after SDK commit; inside the parent they remain preceding overlay changes. Block retry really removes Deadline membership and returns B+1 Service residence; peer Tick outcome stays opaque. Whether the dependency fixture's own prelude mutates must be observed, not inferred from a separate retry fixture |
| FRAME adapter | Public dispatchables are expanded with with_storage_layer. Each partial entry/Deadline/dependency control records its own matching adapter, while the real parent encloses all children in one layer. FRAME's transaction-level key is transient overlay state: source-equivalent child adapters do not make their glue disjoint or additive |

The specific successful entry does not by itself manufacture an altered starting state root. Even with a matched root/value state, a partial SDK control restarts its recorder and preloads its whitelist after setup commit, whereas the actual parent retains preceding reads. Conversely, a real Deadline mutation can change both overlay lineage and trie paths before dependency work. Namespace metadata, saved proof equality and post-wipe TOP restoration decide neither distinction. Never price a standalone increment as an intra-parent increment merely because helper code and local ledger deltas match.

Installed controls are not a matched-cut experiment: public c1 retains reference activity, Deadline prepares its own populated/deep retry fixture, and dependency prepares empty/member/completion source classes. They do not assert one common pre-entry snapshot. Dependency c2 additionally advances its one member in setup; that authored source class is not evidence of the same parent invocation doing that earlier work. Existing four-dispatch Wasm c0 executes the frozen parent and reaches neither Deadline nor dependency.

A useful smallest falsifier is a paired native prefix on one existing lawful non-deep retry fixture, retaining its neighbors, peer clock and B+1. Bind one original root and compare entry→Deadline inside one FRAME layer without an intervening commit against the corresponding standalone cut. Keep original/overlay changes, recorder start/end, transaction-key handling and post-prefix repair separate; do not create production instrumentation or count adapter glue twice. This identifies a finite test-consumer question, not an admitted implementation/collection plan: the existing fixture/recorder owner must first provide a supported seam. It proves neither dependency transfer nor full public/FRAME/default-Wasm qualification.

Source anchors are lib.rs::begin_mandatory_prepass/service_prepass_deadlines/service_prepass_dependency_scan/execute_mandatory_prepass/process_next_due_block_deadline, scheduler.rs::current_scheduler_tick, benchmarking.rs::mandatory_prepass_*_control/prepare_retry_deadline_service, configs/actor_config.rs::DeosActorPrepassContext and configs/mod.rs::BlockResourceBudgetValue. Locked FRAME49 transactional.rs and procedural41 pallet/expand/call.rs own the layer; benchmarking50 v1.rs::on_before_start owns setup commit/whitelist/counter order. ## Literal Idle Plus Finalize Consumer Contract

Status: METHOD ONLY accepted and frozen by coordinator after independent review, before implementation. Source authorship, compile/lint, source acceptance and execution are separate gates; method acceptance authorizes no body, SDK or collector execution. No execution result is accepted in this section. Question: can one extra-only componentless control execute the literal successful breaker-frozen Hooks::on_idle → Hooks::on_finalize workflow, including the real phase/publication/consumption work omitted by the legacy bodies? Proposed name: block_resource_idle_finalize_control, Measured PoV mode, empty component metadata. It is a B+F inclusive source control, not a pure finalizer, a maximum or a new WeightInfo owner.

The owning sources are lib.rs::on_idle/on_finalize/settle_on_idle_control/mandatory_actor_control_envelopes and types/resource.rs::reserve_mandatory_actor_control/begin_drain/finish_drain/finalized_snapshot. Protocol authority remains resource-policy §§4/6 and the Actors specification, not this dossier. Existing helper prepare_breaker_resource_state is reused; no copied scheduler/finalizer, new production hook, recorder observer or std-only private-state facade is needed because both Hooks are public.

Preparation, outside recording: admit only a parent whose breaker is already false and whose scheduler time does not need rewinding to the helper's Tick2. The helper's repeated false write is then idempotent initialization, not a thaw of an existing frozen host. It advances through the existing clock/context helper and calls the real payload-free actor_prepass. In the reference host that context helper writes synthetic ValidationData; this is benchmark-context conformance, not relay authentication, Executive ancestry or admitted network history. Keep all legitimate prefix effects, fees, clocks and peers.

After the helper returns current-block ExternalPhase with its frozen budget, zero reservations and no optional halt, obtain T::GlobalBreakerOrigin::try_successful_origin and invoke public set_global_circuit_breaker(true). Refuse unavailable authority rather than injecting Root or calling apply_global_circuit_breaker directly. Capture the resulting state S only after this setter: breaker and halt must both be true, block/phase/budget/usage must remain the same except that halt, and no reservation may remain. No direct CurrentBlockResourceState write, peer clearing, fake aging, fee override or recomputed budget is admitted.

Let H be the checked sum of current scheduler_on_idle_base and block_resource_finalize declarations; refuse overflow or H=0. Choose finite hook argument R=T::ActorOnIdleReserve::get(), not Weight::MAX or an invented margin. Let C be frozen Actor Control limit minus S.usage.actor_control_used, checked on both axes. Require H≤min(R,C), and checked U_control+H. Require previous telemetry absent or tagged with a different block, without deleting it; otherwise the fixture does not establish fresh publication. These are branch-admission conditions, not claims that H physically bounds the body or that FRAME supplied this remainder in a real block.

The recorded block contains exactly two calls in order, with only a local returned-Weight assignment: w=Hooks::on_idle(now,R); Hooks::on_finalize(now). No inspection, assertions, encoding, root calculation, iterator, reset/commit, observer, extra transaction wrapper or recorder restart is inserted between those calls. The finalizer's own current-state take, telemetry read and validity assertion are part of the measured target and must not be removed as verification. A panic/trap, zero/refused idle or missing finalization is failure, not a typed successful refusal. The finalizer has no second Weight return; on_idle prepays the fixed H once.

| Verification after recording | Exact required fact |
| --- | --- |
| Returned control | w=H on both axes; this is booked Weight, never a measured RefTime claim |
| Marker | CurrentBlockResourceState is absent after exactly one finalizer; do not recreate it or run a second finalizer |
| Fresh telemetry | Current block tag, S.budget unchanged, fixed_reserved equals its fixed envelope, halt=true |

Executive::initialize_block_impl calls System::reset_events first (frame-executive49:625–674). System::reset_events kills Events/EventCount and clears EventTopics (frame-system49:2265); System::initialize itself does not. Execute/finalize paths set Finalization, run PostTransactions, then all idle hooks and all finalizers (Executive:700–720,784–851). OnIdle tuple rotation/shrinking remainder is hooks.rs:126–150. This source establishes lifecycle/order, not reachability of a particular headroom failure, event-prefix maximum or a sound reset/common-input price. Reset/old-topic cleanup belongs to the prefix, not a new F charge.

The first uncovered event-tail transfer is the complete System event-append input retained by or attributable to B within H, not pure finalizer F. deposit_event passes empty topics; deposit_event_indexed reads phase/count, checked-increments count and appends Events (System49:1880–1929). These three storages are marked whitelist_storage (1047–1096), propagated by procedural41 pallet_struct.rs:181–197. Runtime apis.rs:496 adds the benchmark-only selector before dispatch; v1.rs:1052–1104 then adds its common keys and uses the live effective W. No exact W vector/cardinality/flags is claimed from these layers alone.

BenchmarkRecording::start runs the callback before saving start proof/time; stop saves body end and diff_pov saturating-subtracts (benchmarking50 utils.rs:407–455). Its callback commits setup, prefills every effective-W key and resets only DB counters (v1.rs:1076–1104). client-db0.55 read_write_count excludes whitelisted trackers (bench.rs:558–579). With recording enabled, prefill puts the selected authenticated input in the recorder baseline; it does not make those inputs intrinsically absent or cost-free. Missing start/end recording falls through diff_pov().unwrap_or_default() in v1.rs; fallback zero is not evidence of B=0. Missing baseline/common-input qualification cannot be repaired by calling the returned increment the full event witness. No numerical baseline or proof value was observed here.

The smallest transfer falsifier is already source-level: equal Events bytes/semantic output do not imply the same append owner path. Ext::storage_append passes a backend-storage initializer to the overlay (ext.rs:461–477); changeset.rs:401–505 invokes it with no per-key entry, but uses Remove/Append/Set branches for existing versions, with different init/clone behavior. SDK Ext::commit merges outstanding transactions, drains the overlay and commits/reopens the backend (ext.rs:593–615; bench.rs:489–530). Prefill reads do not recreate that live entry (ext.rs:137–145). A lawful earlier same-block event can leave Append, whereas commit-then-append takes the initializer path. This rejects unconditional transfer from equal snapshots; it proves no CPU direction, proof discount, required old payload, repair freedom or numerical underfit.

The closed frozen control has no starvation-event body and therefore is not invalidated by its setup phase or event-input omissions.
