## Event Append Caller Input Contract

Status: METHOD ACCEPTED ONLY. Source/compile and the finite native result have separate accepted dispositions below; no production qualification follows from this method. Consumer: BACKLOG.md Actors/P28-08/Prepass Coordination. The method-review task stopped at acceptance; the explicit 2026-10-29 Grow Loop restart admits the bounded source/compile cohort, not production changes or native execution before source acceptance. The global [State Witness Accounting contract](../../../../../docs/deos/state-witness-accounting.contract.en.md) retains original/overlay/repair and resource-accounting authority.

**Purpose and scope:** Establish one finite native result: retained original inputs authenticate a selected read at R0 and support application of the complete net delta of the selected interval.

The interval is: committed block24 state → target block25 prefix → Actors on_idle → Actors on_finalize → post-Actors state. All original inputs are authenticated against one base root, R0. The post-Actors root is an output, not another original-input root. Successful delta replay does not prove completeness of every input required to re-execute the interval.

**Fixture and entry conditions:**

- Use one existing development preset, ordinary runtime authority and public clock, breaker, Prepass and Hook operations. Preserve legitimate residents.
- Produce Starving24 through 24 completed halt-only observations. Reset System events before each new System initialization, complete Timestamp once per preparation block, and commit only between completed blocks. Do not inject counter age, readiness, resource state or event padding.
- Export the completed fixture without normalizing its block context. Independently construct its original database and R0; bind the fixture, native host/profile, trie layout and hash.
- Start one fresh recording host from that fixture. Use an explicitly empty tracked-key whitelist and no SDK prefill. Retain the actual baseline; an empty whitelist does not establish zero baseline.
- Prepare target25 through canonical event reset, monotone clock/context advancement, public freeze before Prepass, public thaw retaining the optional-work halt, and transition to Finalization. Require valid current-block authority, breaker false, optional work halted, and sufficient allowance for housekeeping on both Weight axes.

**Capture boundary:**

- Require an available recorder and retain its initial baseline snapshot.
- From target event reset until the post-Hooks capture, perform no harness reads of Events/EventCount, whole-state enumeration, root construction, commit/reopen or unrelated semantic diagnostics. Ordinary reads performed by the selected public operations remain allowed.
- Add no harness transaction around the target interval. Public setup transactions must return to the host's outer scope.
- Call Actors on_idle and on_finalize consecutively, without intermediate observation. Immediately retain the recorder snapshot as P_body; it includes baseline and prefix inputs.
- Construct the post-Actors root and immediately retain P_root, before semantic queries or state enumeration. Timestamp completion is outside this witness interval.
- After capture, enumerate complete live state, including overlay updates and deletions. Handle the empty key and reject child storage. Independently rebuild the post-Actors root and derive the complete insert/update/delete delta from the original fixture.

**Witness construction and acceptance:**

- Classify recorded nodes by exact membership in the independently constructed R0 database. Exclude computed-only output nodes.
- Using only the original subset of P_body, authenticate IdleStarvationState at R0 as Starving24.
- Identify original repair inputs as original nodes present in P_root but absent from P_body. Keep their attribution separate from Hook execution inputs.
- Using only the original subset of P_root, apply the complete delta at R0 and require equality with the independently rebuilt post-Actors root.
- Post-capture checks must confirm the selected halt-only path, Alerted25, the expected detection-event suffix, valid finalized telemetry and consumed resource marker. Reconstruct event-prefix geometry from actual post-capture records and source-derived append semantics; do not require a hard-coded total prefix length or silently discard unexpected events.
- Missing authentication or replay inputs fail the witness. Do not supplement it with unrecorded nodes from the original database or computed outputs.

**Result limits and stop rule:** PASS establishes only the selected original read and complete net-delta replay under the bound native context. It does not establish full execution-input completeness, payload necessity/minimality, SDK transfer, default-host equivalence, complete Executive history, physical maxima, production Weight, fees or reclaim. Stop after one conforming case or the first concrete blocker. No paired arm, body-only sufficiency experiment, parameter sweep or confidence rerun is included.

## Inherent-Only Bare Admission

Status: APPROVED REFERENCE POLICY ONLY / BOUNDED IMPLEMENTATION SCOPE CLOSED. The renewed user direction selects the next System phase/Shared gap. **Decision and API constraint:** Runtime49 transaction_extension/mod.rs:425–445 returns unit from bare prepare and passes no reservation Pre token into bare post-dispatch. The current resource state stores aggregate usage/outstanding count, not a retained external token; that count is not interchangeable with owned reservation authority. Preserving external bare upgrades would require a separately designed token carrier and its complete accounting/binding; pre-dispatch final settlement or reconstructing authority from a count is not accepted. The existing operator helper already calls signAndSubmit. The smaller reference contract admits bare only through the three inherent providers and routes external upgrades through the existing ordinary signed pipeline.

**Capability boundary:** A code hash accepted by the SDK System unsigned validator no longer grants bare transport in the DEOS Executive. Any signed relayer account may still submit the authorized call without governance/Root privilege, subject to account/nonce/upfront funding, ExternalPhase and complete Shared admission/settlement. SDK authorization, code/version checks, pending-code semantics and fee/refund policy are unchanged by this source patch; an unfunded account-free bare relay is intentionally no longer supported. This is a reference-runtime transport restriction, not an upstream pallet change or a general theorem that every bare call is an inherent. Resource-policy §6 owns that intended contract; script help/authority text and scripts README describe signed relay.

## Inherent Byte and Admission-Guard Accounting

Status: ACCEPTED FINITE COUNTER / PROSPECTIVE OWNER MAP ONLY / BOUNDED ACCOUNTING SCOPE CLOSED. Selected scope is mandatory-inherent byte accounting and the guard's complete cost owner, not physical collection or a generated price. The accepted inherent-only policy and prior native campaigns remain closed. Current production guard a838ddfb, configf76dc820, meter7b0d9dfb and numeric weights are unchanged in this slice; only a project-owned native test and its module attachment extend executable evidence.

**Units and source:** Define L_I as the sum of full encoded unchecked lengths of the three inherents, including SCALE framing; H_0 as System::block_size immediately after actual Executive initialization; D as the encoded item lengths of later runtime-emitted digest logs. In the witnessed no-external-call cases, BlockSize after Prepass equals H_0+L_I+D, not L_I alone. Executive49:615–622 filters the supplied header to pre-runtime digests before System initialization. System49:1970–1998 seeds BlockSize with encoded empty-header size plus that digest's encoded size, not the exact complete incoming header encoding. System49:2120–2125 adds deposited log-item lengths. ParachainSystem0.31:757–763 deposits its relay-parent-storage-root item. Counter values are neither automatically billed FRAME ProofSize nor network PoV/state proof.

| Item | FRAME / frozen owner | Required accounting boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Three Mandatory bases | Fixed constructor already includes each once; CheckWeight charges each inherent's base. | Do not add another base merely because the guard is now explicit. |
| L_I encoded length | CheckWeight adds (0,len) per inherent; generic bare reclaim retains it. Configured fixed construction names no explicit current-length term. | Name it once in authoritative settled-prefix or a qualified inclusive fixed owner. Absence of a named term is not numerical undercharge proof. |
| H_0 and D counter additions | Initial/append BlockSize updates are distinct from per-extrinsic FRAME length charges. Later signed proof replacement can consume BlockSize in its floor. | Keep header/digest and signed outer-correction ownership separate; do not copy aggregate BlockSize into L_I. |
| Provisional Prepass maximum | Bare preparation temporarily books the maximum dispatch declaration. | Exclude only that dispatch reservation from settled fixed truth; retain its base/encoded bytes and later actual Actor-domain return. |
| Admission guard | Executive's bare application invokes it before extension preparation. The source calls only three provider classifiers, each a call-variant match. | Complete fixed outer-admission owner, no extra Actor fee. No direct FRAME debit or persistent access in this source is not zero physical cost. |

**Native falsifier:** tests/production_block_replay/inherent_accounting.rs019c0de974491ad3b8e1a486259cc01d9f37b5aa7e306f63494d86ddb6dd8b4c, attached by replay67341c43cd2db86eb3171b94defb113e030ece49719a70d15f332b1c1f5b78c8, executes three actual runtime-created inherents after real Executive initialization on zero-added-Actor fixtures. For every inherent it probes the real guard and bare prepare in a rollback-only transaction, asserting unchanged guard root/counters and exact checked preparation sum: prior Weight + call/extension declaration + class base + (0,full encoded length). It restores the root, applies the real Executive call, checks length retention and separate runtime-log counter increments, then completes native finalization. This is a counter/phase test, not whole-block import or physical instrumentation. Manual guard/prepare probes are extra test work, not a claim that valid-block execution charges them twice.

Parent/header payloads are fixture operands, not new admission caps or public-ancestry proofs. The parent change grows FRAME-charged inherent bytes with constant configured freeze; the current-header change grows initial length counter with unchanged registered Weight and inherent bytes. None of these values is a maximum or proof that fixed slack safely covers every admitted context. Nor does the finite counter difference establish a universal ordering between BlockSize and FRAME ProofSize.

**Guard cost boundary:** The canonical successful block has three required bare inherents and checked_extrinsic49 applies the guard once per bare application. Builder and import are separate executions, not six on-chain charges; direct pool rejection is not admitted block work. The wrapper and Timestamp49:328/ParachainSystem0.31:1146/Actors:14040 classifiers perform variant matching without payload traversal, encoding, cloning or direct storage calls. **Pricing-method gate:** First prove that a complete existing outer-admission owner dominates current guarded execution across the actual three families and admitted input/host classes, or qualify a complete inclusive replacement and retire its contained charges once. Separate Prepass Actor Control/effects from generic outer admission; do not move Actor body work into fixed pricing or subtract an unqualified helper timing. The public benchmark script currently exposes pallet collection, not a prepared complete-inherent/guard calibration. Canonical production Wasm is absent. ## Settled-Prefix Freeze Integration Design

Status: ACCEPTED CONDITIONAL DESIGN ONLY / NOT IMPLEMENTED / DESIGN SCOPE CLOSED. User selected the next design step after accepted byte/guard reconciliation. This slice changes no runtime, price, benchmark method, storage, ABI, metadata or artifact. Root BACKLOG.md P28-19 is the only open queue; this design records inputs, boundaries and falsifiers, not another execution plan or qualified numeric owner.

**Reuse and entry:** Actors lib.rs:12666–12730 calls BlockResourceBudgetProvider::for_prepass after context/duplicate checks and before the first Actor reservation. Generic resource.rs:624–653 already provides from_settled_prefix(M, maximum_fixed, prefix, tail, numerator, denominator), with checked addition/subtraction and a host-fixed ceiling. No new allocator is needed. Reference runtime configs/mod.rs:402–412 has only the cfg(test) comparison override; production inherits the configured default. A future adapter must execute the real production path in ordinary native tests rather than hide it behind that test override.

**Candidate normalization:** Let W_entry be authoritative FRAME total at the authenticated SDK application boundary, and D_prepass the current bare Prepass dispatch declaration (currently M, zero extension Weight). Define P=W_entry−D_prepass with checked component-wise arithmetic. This candidate preserves every initialization/context registration and all three retained Mandatory bases/encoded lengths, including Prepass overhead; it does not add L_I or aggregate BlockSize again. P includes any future work prepaid by its existing complete owners. It is not a physical-work observation. The accepted native probes establish the preparation bookkeeping, not complete registration sufficiency or authenticated entry for every caller.

**Entry proof is mandatory:** Do not subtract M from an arbitrary counter or accept a direct call lacking that reservation. Before normalization, establish one current canonical Prepass application, correct block/context, no existing current-block freeze, and exactly its one provisional declaration in the recorded total. Prefer a bounded proof from existing System/extrinsic context and the admitted owner map; if those facts do not suffice, select an explicitly qualified bounded entry witness rather than add a shadow ledger. Current for_prepass arguments alone contain no dispatch-info/token proof. Overflow/saturation, missing or inconsistent entry and unknown registrations cannot create a credit. System49 CheckWeight uses saturating accrual for an unlimited class; syntactically successful checked subtraction does not prove that the input counter was never saturated. Establish non-saturation from the admitted upstream path, not arithmetic success alone.

**Single freeze:** Let R be the checked sum of complete fixed work still outstanding and not already prepaid in P. Let F_admitted be a sound pre-execution worst-case fixed ceiling, not a clipped copy of the current configured sum. Compute exactly one budget through from_settled_prefix(M,F_admitted,P,R,1,3), so F=P+R. Reuse the existing begin_prepass monotonic checks and current-block state. Subsequent Actor Control, Shared admission, Drain and final reconciliation reuse that immutable budget. A late tail consuming less than reserved never expands it. Actor H, Prepass body and Task effects remain their own existing domains, not components of R.

| Owner / current source | Completed or prepaid at entry | Remaining fixed treatment / qualification |
| --- | --- | --- |
| FRAME base and three bare overheads | Executive49:625–667 registers base/initialization; CheckWeight charges all inherent bases/lengths. | P retains them once. Guard/outer and complete block/finalization domination still need qualification; base declarations are not physical evidence. |
| Timestamp49:217–229 | on_initialize explicitly returns WeightInfo::on_finalize; dispatch adds set weight. | Do not reserve finalization again when this complete source owner is qualified. Prepayment comes from initialization, not dispatch alone. |
| Governance local lib.rs:1882 | Initialization returns pending enactment plus the selected epoch-service charge. | That service phase is closed; retain its charged owner, not an additional unused configured service maximum. Full current-owner/domain/error qualification remains required. |
| MessageQueue53:682–700 | Initialization returns configured service execution; IdleMaxServiceWeight=() in runtime. | No second idle service reserve. Context enqueue can create next-block work, not a new current idle lane; busy processor coverage remains open. |
| SessionRotation local lib.rs:48–55 | Nonrotation returns zero; rotation returns one complete R after callbacks. SDK Session50:671 uses NeverEndSession. | No future Session rotation tail. Preserve accepted single registration; complete physical/LP/failure coverage remains separately open. |
| Aura49 and session handler | Initialization and any authority-update callbacks execute before freeze. | No duplicate callback charge. Digest/authority applicability and complete current owner must be qualified. |
| AuraExt0.31:70–80 | Initialization returns one DB read. | Finalization separately reads Aura authorities and writes its cache. Current configured2reads/1write is not prepaid by the1read return and is not a complete physical proof. Retain a qualified disjoint tail or bind one inclusive prepaid owner. |
| Authorship49:55–65 / CollatorSelection31:945–961 | Authorship returns zero; note_author callback separately registers its business declaration. | Discovery/cache creation are not proved contained by that callback; cache deletion occurs later. Repair/qualify the complete registration and tail before treating P as complete; do not invent coverage or double-add an inclusive wrapper. |
| ParachainSystem0.31:562–667 | Initialization explicitly prepays finalizer terms, including announced HRMP handling; validation body adds direct registrations. | Do not reserve those named prepaid terms twice. Complete admission/bytes/UMP-HRMP growth/upgrade/fault-tail coverage is not established by DB counts or the prepayment comment. |
| XCM30:1016–1050 | Discovery and optional lazy migration run in initialization and return their charge. | They are not future idle work. Full discovery/input/migration reachability and physical owner remain gates; no duplicate XCM migration reserve. |
| XCMP0.33:276–290 | Its lazy migration is in on_idle, after freeze. | Keep its qualified current upper in R unless absence is mechanically proved; source bookkeeping includes it in the existing fixed envelope. Do not add it a second time to that same envelope. |
| Post-inherent/poll/post-transaction | System config inherits unit callbacks and unit MultiBlockMigrator; selected27 pallet roots define no custom on_poll body. | Preserve Executive glue in its complete base owner; empty callback bodies do not prove zero whole-phase cost. A changed binding reopens the row. |
| System finalization and upgrade application | Some work belongs to block/base or explicit earlier prepayments. Executive49:784–800 still runs late hooks, pending-code application and System finalize. | Current source does not qualify every such tail. An ordinary no-upgrade profile needs an explicit source/state premise; an upgrade-capable profile retains complete admitted late-work ownership. |

**Prefix completeness is not optional:** P is authoritative charged Weight only after all reached registrations are classified and the owners dominate their intended work. Unknown past discovery/guard/context work cannot be hidden as a future R item or granted zero. Correct a deficient registration at its actual owner, or establish a qualified inclusive owner with contained charges retired once. The table's prepayment facts do not qualify physical maxima, state-proof bounds, failure suffixes or node-side outer correction. Conservative qualified sums need not claim disjoint trie payloads; overlap gives no automatic discount.

**Static admission before effects:** Prove F_admitted<=floor(M/4), complete worst admitted context/service/tail composition and mandatory H/maximum-Step fit before enabling execution. A quiet P+R below the quarter does not repair an overcommitted busy profile. The current configured pair already exceeds the quarter and is not that certificate. Do not pass min(configured,quarter), reduce required work, substitute a quiet observation, or use a rejection after initialization/context effects as the configuration fix. Then require P+R<=F_admitted at freeze. Provider errors fail closed without silently falling back to configured allocation; that refusal is not the static admission proof.

**Worst guarantee versus quiet allocation:** Configured guarantees must remain derived from the admitted worst-case F_admitted. A smaller current F may expand only that block's allocation through the existing exact thirds; it must not authorize a larger persistent Step. begin_mandatory_prepass already checks equal M, no larger F, and no smaller Control/Actor/User guarantees. Keep class caps, FRAME idle invocation and signed outer-proof/headroom obligations separate: one correct economic freeze does not prove actual FRAME H handoff.

**Bounded read truth:** Current BlockResourceBudget stores the total fixed envelope and discards its prefix/tail constructor operands; FinalizedBlockResourceSnapshot::fixed_reserved returns that sum. These surfaces cannot currently expose the required charged-prefix/outstanding-reserve distinction. A later implementation must retain the minimal immutable decomposition inside the existing frozen-state owner and derive read-only projections, not create a second authority or reconstruct history from a later shrinking FRAME counter. Any necessary SCALE/API/projection change belongs to the coherent metadata/client/Wasm binding gate; no field is added by this design.

| Obligation | Smallest falsifier / existing executable owner | Present evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Exact allocator reuse | resource.rs settled_prefix_allocation_uses_the_existing_exact_thirds_partition and independent overflow/exhaustion tests | Existing test source inspected; not rerun or newly accepted here. |
| Actual entry normalization | Existing full_executive_prepass_prefix_excludes_its_provisional_reservation plus missing/wrong/duplicate-entry regressions | Retained finite preparation evidence; production authentication/complete-owner proof still required. |
| Byte/prepayment exclusivity | Accepted inherent byte witness; remove one contained base/length or double-add a prepaid tail | Finite byte units accepted; qualified complete prefix/tail partition remains open. |
| Tail branch coverage | Timestamp prepayment, AuraExt/Authorship late work, busy XCM/upgrade/idle source cases | Source placement reconciled; complete tail coefficients/host/data domains unqualified. |
| Static quarter and Step safety | system_quarter_preserves_thirds_and_forward_borrowing_across_block_phases plus real worst-bound configuration/context admission | Generic algebra exists; current reference configuration fails the known quarter requirement. |
| One freeze and projection truth | Source for_prepass before first reservation; compare retained P/R projection through Base/external/Drain/finalize and reject second freeze/credit | Current state enforces one allocation; complete projected decomposition and production adapter not implemented. |

**Decision and exact next gate:** Reuse the existing provider/constructor; do not install the arithmetic-only adapter now. First close missing charged-prefix and unprepaid-tail owners, qualify a worst admissible fixed ceiling/Step guarantee, and establish canonical-entry normalization plus bounded projection binding. Then one source-fix cohort can wire the production provider and its adversarial regressions. Pricing requires separately prepared ordinary-Wasm/host/whole-owner evidence, not execution of a new helper benchmark here. No native/build/measurement campaign or production mutation accompanies this design; independent review accepts the conditional algorithm and exclusions without mandatory corrections, not implemented conformance or permission to wire the provider.

## Prepass Reservation Causality and Retained Counter Lineage

Status: ACCEPTED SOURCE-BOUND ARITHMETIC ONLY / SOURCE COHORT CLOSED / NO PRODUCTION ADAPTER. Locked scope is P28-19 upstream entry/reservation and counter-history reasoning on the accepted Timestamp-context correction. This source-only cohort changes no Rust, weights, fields, artifacts or tests. It does not replay the accepted native cases or qualify full physical prefix coverage.

**Domain:** A successful ordinary reference-runtime Prepass entered by the selected Executive from an admitted parent/prefix, with the current locked code/configuration, not a direct native helper call, a failed builder candidate retained without rollback, an arbitrary host/Root storage replacement, or another embedding. Future callbacks, private Timestamp lifecycle changes, runtime/SDK replacement or altered extensions reopen this proof. Actual ordinary-Wasm/host binding is still missing. This is source reasoning about registered scalar charges, never proof that those charges dominate performed work.

**Causal chain:** Executive49:856–909 derives DispatchInfo from the same checked extrinsic whose full encoded bytes it stages. Runtime49 checked_extrinsic.rs:124–142 runs the bound unsigned owner, then bare preparation, then dispatch. For the only recognized Actors inherent, source lib.rs:13999 gives D=M and zero bare extension Weight. CheckWeight49:132–145 reads current counters, computes their accrual and writes the result without an intervening callback. Its current bundle is q=D+B+(0,4)=(2000125000000ps,10485764bytes), using the selected Mandatory base and full encoded empty-Prepass length. Both components fit u64. No scalar match or hypothetical accumulated M is used to identify this particular reservation.

**Continuity to provider:** Current Actor entry checks the update-marker/ValidationData context, bounded generated envelopes and duplicate/stale state before for_prepass. These current helpers/weights read or calculate; no FRAME-counter write is between that SDK preparation and the provider on the successful path. Duplicate current state returns before invoking it. Current source sites construct the retained current-block resource state in begin_mandatory_prepass; the additional scheduler simulation construction is local and called inside execution.rs:2173's explicit outer Rollback. Governance breaker handling only halts an existing same-block state; it cannot create admission authority for an external-before-Prepass transaction.

**Context-before-entry:** In an admitted prefix the fresh Timestamp marker and ValidationData require successful context operations before Prepass. Bare admission names only these three providers. Timestamp and validation duplicates fail their mandatory call; a second Prepass fails the existing state check. Signed/general external preparation cannot create a fresh UserDispatch reservation before Prepass: the meter checks current-block state and source has no earlier retained creator. General inherent-prefix and mandatory-failure rules remain Executive-owned. Consequently the selected valid path reaches Prepass after the two context inherents, in either relative order. This source inference is not a generic promise of the for_prepass arguments or a replacement for later adapter-specific entry verification.

**Coverage method:** Counter-type/mutator and raw-write searches covered runtime source/configs/generated weights and the eight local pallet source roots, plus19 selected SDK pallet src roots: System49, ParachainSystem0.31, Timestamp49, ParachainInfo0.29, Reclaim0.12, Balances51, Payment50, Authorship49, CollatorSelection31, Session50, Aura49, AuraExt0.31, XCMP0.33, XCM30, CumulusXcm0.28, MQ53, Assets54, AssetConversion32 and Preimage50. Inline cfg(test) matches were classified, not mistaken for production. Callback escape analysis uses concrete Governance/MessageQueue/XCM/asset/fee/session adapters rather than pallet names alone. Name search is evidence for the map, not a formal call-graph verifier; independent review must challenge missing aliases, raw writers or reachable debits.

**Retained-lineage lemma, component-wise:** Let U=u64::MAX. Starting at reset0, an accrue edge maps x to min(U,x+a), with a>=0. A full rollback selects an ancestor snapshot; retained history is the surviving root-to-current path after abandoned suffixes are removed. If its current component x<U and all surviving mutation edges are accruals, no earlier node on that retained path reached U: accrual cannot leave U. Therefore all surviving additions in that component were exact. Apply independently to both Weight axes; before Prepass all observed production registrations are Mandatory and the other classes start/stay zero, so total introduces no cross-class saturation. Current-MAX rejection plus this source-specific operation map, not subtraction success alone, establishes the stated arithmetic condition.

**Important counterexample boundary:** A temporary branch may reach U, then roll back completely to a finite ancestor; it does not falsify that lemma. Conversely, U followed by a surviving reduce/set can yield a finite current counter and defeats it. The source map must exclude the latter, not pretend that a current value proves all history. Failed/refused/rolled-back work still needs its complete incurred-work owner; deleting a branch from registered ancestry neither refunds its CPU nor certifies any fixed/Actor price. This distinction preserves the existing committed-prefix/rollback accounting contracts.

**Normalization consequence:** Under this exact source/domain and a non-MAX accepted snapshot, W_entry is the exact retained sum of registered charges. Removing only current D yields P=W_entry−D, preserving its base/4encoded bytes, the two prior inherent charges and all initialization/direct/prepaid registrations. Unknown or underpriced work is not thereby charged, and none may be placed in a fake future R. The callback interface still carries no generic bare reservation token: source-bound causality is not general external-token reconstruction. No production normalizer, guard removal or quiet allocator is authorized by this proof candidate.

## Session Rotation Registered-Weight Source Reconciliation

Status: VERIFIED PRE-PATCH SOURCE/DECLARATION COORDINATE; numerical comparison below belongs to config30651120, before the implementation checkpoint. It is not the current manager's registration sum. No native/benchmark execution, measurements, Rust/generated-code changes, historical rerun or independent-review claim. The comparison is the sum of registration operands for one completed non-saturating scheduled rotation, not complete block usage, physical work or a reachable H failure.

**Exact call chain:** Runtime Period=3600/Offset=0 selects the separate SessionRotation hook; Session itself uses NeverEndSession. Its binding invokes Session::rotate_session once and returns R (configs/mod.rs:387–429,508–514,818–831; session-rotation lib.rs:48–56). Executive49:524–545 registers that return AFTER the callback completes. Session50:768–875 orders before-ending/end -> start_session(i+1) -> new_session(i+2) -> Aura handler. Runtime end_session is empty; SessionKeys selects Aura alone. Aura48:358–399 and System49:2120–2125 add no direct Weight registration; a changed authority digest can affect BlockSize, which is not a separate immediate rotation Weight charge. Authorship's note_author and the SDK CollatorSelection SessionManager implementation are different owners, not additional calls on this bound path.

**Current declaration inputs:** Runtime uses local Session/Staking generated weights and local RocksDB25m/read,100m/write. SecurityRewardClaimHorizon=12, so callbacks pass r=14 (staking_config.rs:27–31,477–513). Exact BigInt arithmetic includes all generated DB terms; it evaluates declarations, not actual database operations or CPU.

| Symbol / declaration | RefTime ps | ProofSize bytes |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| R = rotate_session = SessionRotationFixedWeight | 4192598000 | 315835 |
| S = settle_due_native_security_reward(14) | 13707532970 | 290294 |
| C = contract_native_security_obligations | 523258000 | 14309 |
| K = cancel_native_security_epoch_plan | 234848000 | 3534 |

**Joint production branches:** RuntimeNativeSecurityModeProvider returns TrustedSet whenever runtime-benchmarks is absent; the LP storage override/read exists only under that feature (staking_config.rs:36–69). Mode is not changed by the inspected callbacks. start_session always registers S before settlement; its Err branch registers K then returns, while Ok registers C in TrustedSet. A contraction Err still retains C. Session then invokes new_session regardless of that local return; TrustedSet returns Invulnerables before either LP registration. Thus C and K are alternatives, not additive peers (configs/mod.rs:675–814).

| Completed TrustedSet rotation branch | Registration sum | Sum RefTime ps | Sum ProofSize bytes | Exact excess over R |
| --- | --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| Settlement Ok, contraction Ok or Err | R+S+C | 18423388970 | 620438 | (14230790970 ps,304603 bytes) |
| Settlement Err, cancellation Ok or Err | R+S+K | 18134978970 | 609663 | (13942380970 ps,293828 bytes) |

The genesis-empty/no-raw-mutation selected predicate permits the first branch: retention finds no due pot, contraction has no active/planned obligation, but both registrations remain. This reuses integration's existing Session preservation relation, not a new block/history witness. The second row is an explicit source alternative, NOT newly proved reachable through ordinary production history. C dominates K in both axes, so R+S+C is a conservative declared aggregate across these TrustedSet source outcomes. Neither outcome is dominated by R alone.

**Excluded LP composition:** Only a different/benchmark-overridden mode can reach LP readiness. After start_session, LP new_session contributes zero when not Ready, O(p,14) on a Ready opening attempt, and O(p,14)+N(removed,candidates) only when opening succeeds; a local settlement Err/cancel does not skip new_session. There is no trusted C on an LP start path. p<=100 and removed<=candidates<=100 are typed bounds, not simultaneous-attainment evidence. No LP maximum is selected: the current O benchmark advertises r<=13 while this caller passes14 (and p may be0), so extrapolation is not qualification. This inactive-domain limitation neither changes the ordinary result nor authorizes an LP benchmark campaign.

**Decision and owning correction:** SessionRotationFixedWeight=R does NOT dominate the complete registered ordinary rotation in either axis. The local positive residual is exactly S+C on the selected successful TrustedSet path, or S+K on its failure alternative. P28-19/reference runtime composition owns reconciliation of that return/direct-registration relationship under Session Rotation specification §2's single complete-call owner. Merely adding the residual to FixedBlockWeight would not resolve duplicated accounting of execution already intended inside R. Nor may callback charges be blindly deleted for other consumers. No correction or replacement coefficient is installed here.

**Reuse/limits:** Integration's Observed original Actor phase relations already includes TrustedSet settlement/contraction in its conditional bare FRAME upper; no old whole-block figure is recomputed, rebound or promoted. Its finite empty/reconciled/no-extra-Control-debit predicates do not establish universal production H headroom. Physical complete R/H/fixed bounds, global fixed reserve/quarter, bare lengths, outer signed correction and fault suffixes retain their separate owners; no overflow, missed block, CPU gain, fee reduction or production price follows. Production Not Prepared/O43 Proposed; old G protected.
